### 2025-03-23 09:08 — why the Austin Founder Retreat costs $3k
- From: Matt Gray @founderos.com · To: vishnu88varthan @gmail.com
- the event network paradox Hey விஷ்ணு, Have you ever heard of the event network paradox? It's a simple contradiction - The more you charge for an event, the fewer people can afford to come, but the more qualified people actually come. In other words - the fact that the Founder OS Austin Retreat costs $3k to attend might make you think twice about it - but that's the whole point of it! So if the event was, say, $1,000, then it would attract less committed founders and make the event worse for everyone else. (Also, considering the level of venues we're booking for our events, we wouldn't even bre
